### Pattern health (per recurring pattern: last swept, hits that sweep, consecutive clean sweeps; detail in the sweep log below)
_(**S211 exercised NO ACTIVE sweep pattern** — Pass F is a cross-tool consistency audit, not a pattern sweep, and none of its four fixes fell in a registered class. Every pattern's streak and last-swept date is unchanged from S203's sweep; the trio of consequence-critical patterns stays ACTIVE regardless. **No new pattern registered:** the Pass F divergence is one shipped micro-feature that has not been replicated, which is F's own remit, not a recurring code shape.)_
- **error-status-clobbered-by-a-later-routine-write** (**NEW, registered S199**): a status/live-region line that correctly reports a FAILURE is then overwritten, on the same code path, by a later **routine** write that assumes the happy state — so the diagnostic exists in the source, fires, and is gone before the user reads it, leaving advice that the failure itself made impossible. Sibling of `wired-then-clobbered label` (S192), but the victim is an **error message** rather than a translated label, and the clobberer is a normal-path status write rather than a JS setter. **Detection: never by reading the code** — both call sites look correct in isolation. Force the failure (block the tool's `data/` corpus with `serviceWorkers:'block'`), then read the tool's OWN `[role=status]`/live-region element at the END of init and compare it against the message the catch set. **Do NOT regex body text for `fail|error|…`** — that produced a false negative here on both carriers (curly apostrophes, and wording like "unavailable"). Swept 2026-08-12 (S199 Pass B), hits: **1** (torah_trainer, fixed b5bd9c0), clean: trope_tutor + dictionary. **Surface = the 3 data-loading tools**; the other 4 request no `data/` corpus at load. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE.
- **animation-outside-its-reduced-motion-block** (**NEW, registered S198**): a CSS `transition`/`animation` declaration whose selector is **not covered by its own page's `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block** — so a reader who asked the OS for less motion still gets that one. CLAUDE.md makes the coverage binding ("if you add an animation to a page, that page needs the reduced-motion block, and your animation must honor it"), which is exactly why a *partial* block is the dangerous shape: the block's presence reads as compliance. **Detection (cheap, mechanical, no rendering needed):** for each file, collect every selector carrying a `transition:`/`animation:` declaration outside the reduced-motion block, then subtract the selectors named inside it; anything left is a candidate. **Confirm at runtime with a control arm** — read `transitionDuration` under `reducedMotion:'reduce'` **and** `'no-preference'`; a probe that reports `0s` in both states is measuring nothing. **The sibling heuristic is what found S198's hit:** elements that *travel together* (a drawer and its backdrop, a modal and its scrim) must be neutralized together, and a block naming one but not the other is the tell. Swept 2026-08-12 (S198): **1 hit / 3 carriers of the settings drawer** — `torah_trainer` (fixed 915526d); `classroom_dashboard` and `trope_tutor` already correct. **Not yet swept suite-wide** — S198 censused only the `.settings-backdrop` shape; the full 11-page selector-subtraction sweep is the next A run's job. ACTIVE, clean streak 0. **⚑ FIRST GENERAL SWEEP 2026-08-12 (S199): 4 hits, all fixed (6fcf9e4).** Surface: all 12 pages, current-state. **Detection is now settled, and two thresholds must be right or the number is meaningless:** run the page under `reducedMotion:'reduce'` and read COMPUTED `transition-duration`/`animation-*` — but (a) **`0.00001s` is the neutralizer idiom (`0.01ms !important`) and counts as COVERAGE, not a hit**, so threshold at >1ms, and (b) bucket by transitioned **property**: explicit `transform`/position/size (and any keyframe animation) is the defect; a hover **colour/opacity** fade is not, and lumping them in inflates ~16 real hits to ~108. Result: keyframe animations already neutralized on **every** page (0 suite-wide); the escapees were 4 explicit `transform` transitions on 4 pages (`404 .home-btn`, `generator .vg-arrow`, `dashboard .day-item`, `dictionary #fsBtn`), each on a page whose block already existed and simply omitted them. **Fix-shape note: `!important` is usually REQUIRED, not stylistic** — a media query adds no specificity, so an equal-specificity rule declared later in the file wins (404's fix parsed fine and did nothing until it was added), and `.vg-arrow` carries its transition as an **inline** style. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **Remaining, deliberately NOT claimed as defects:** the `transition: all` hover declarations on generator/dashboard/dictionary/torah (see Candidates). **S204 detection refinement: the census MUST include bare-duration shorthands — `transition: 0.2s` is implicit `all` and a literal-'all' grep misses it (3 of torah's 8 real sites were this shape, one hiding the toggle-knob translateX, the class's first real motion hit). transition:all arm status after S204: dictionary S203 + generator/torah S204 CLOSED; dashboard is the last arm.** **S205: the transition:all backlog candidate is fully CLOSED (all four arms + flash_cards clean by census); the PATTERN stays ACTIVE for new code.**
- **content-dependent-tour-step-miscounts-the-tour** (**NEW, registered S197**): a guided-tour step whose `target()` only exists once remote/corpus content has rendered. The engines all skip an unresolvable step **silently by design** ("skip gracefully when hidden"), but the step counter is built from the **raw array index**, so on any failed/absent load the number **jumps and over-promises** — the teacher watches steps vanish. **Detection (cheap, ~2 min/tool): walk the real tour on a fresh profile and compare steps SHOWN against `TOUR_STEPS.length`; then re-walk with that tool's own corpus route-aborted.** The second arm is what makes it discriminating — the first arm alone only catches tools whose content is EXTERNAL (torah/Sefaria), and silently passes every tool served from local `data/`. **Fix shape: count by PRESENCE of each target, never visibility** — absent = content never arrived; hidden = a collapsed panel that opens fine. Presence is also the only side-effect-free probe: testing visibility means running every step's `reveal()`, which in the dictionary opens the sidebar and expands panels the tour hasn't reached. FIRST sweep 2026-08-12 (S197 Pass I), all 7 tour-bearing tools: **2 carriers found, both fixed → 0 remaining** (`torah_trainer.html`, `hebrew_dictionary.html`). **trope_tutor is the documented NEGATIVE — it has a content-dependent step too (`#tuCards .tu-card`) yet holds 6/6 with data blocked**, which is what proves the detector isn't just reporting "blocking data breaks tours". The other 4 tools target static chrome only. **Clean streak: 0 (first sweep, hits found).** ACTIVE.
- **per-sample-repaint-of-an-O(n)-live-preview** (**NEW, registered S196**): a continuous gesture — `pointermove`, a slider drag — that rebuilds an **O(n) preview from its whole accumulated buffer once per input SAMPLE** rather than once per animation frame, so the gesture is O(n²) in its own length. The screen cannot show more than one repaint per frame, so every extra rebuild is pure waste, and the cost is invisible at the short strokes/small drags a developer tests with. **Detection:** find the expensive builder call inside a `pointermove`/`oninput` handler where the builder walks a whole array; then, under **PACED** input (yield to rAF between batches — a synchronous dispatch loop starves rAF and fakes a 100× win), count builder invocations against frames. A per-sample handler shows `invocations == accepted samples`; a correct one shows `invocations ≈ frames`. Confirm with a first-10%-vs-last-10% mean ratio per sample — a flat ratio is clean, a rising one is the class. **Fix idiom (already in-tree twice):** `boldGenSet` and `nodeStatusCoords` — coalesce with an rAF token on the gesture object, re-read state at frame time (never snapshot), and guard the callback with `gesture !== g` so an ended gesture paints nothing. **Found + fixed 2026-08-12 (S196, Pass D): 1 hit** — Font Maker's four styled draw pens (2e09c29); its round pen is the same shape but measured under budget and deliberately left (logged as a Candidate with the two inputs that would make it actionable). **⚑ Notable: S85's Pass D fixed this exact shape in the same tool** (Generate Bold's per-frame warning scan), and Draw mode reintroduced it 111 sessions later — so this class recurs *within* a tool as new gesture surfaces are built. Clean streak: 0 (first sweep). Correctness-adjacent (degrades the primary drawing interaction), not consequence-critical.
- **false-positive-validator-on-the-app's-own-content** (**NEW, registered S194**): a QA/lint/warning rule whose **detector is broader than the failure it warns about**, so it fires on artwork or data the app itself ships — the user cannot act on it, cannot clear it, and it is present in **every** project, which is precisely how a panel of real warnings gets trained into background noise. Founding instance: the Font Maker outline scan flagging its own built-in **Comma** and **Semicolon** (`glyphOutlineScan` tested "do two contours' edges cross" while the message promised "can fill **inverted**" — inversion needs *opposite* winding, and both glyphs are all-outer, so nonzero simply unions them). **Detection:** for each validator, run it on a **pristine/empty project** — anything it reports there is by definition not the user's doing. Cheap (one page-evaluate) and it generalizes past contrast/i18n to every warning surface: FM QA, the export guard, the dictionary/flash-cards load states. **Second question once a hit is found:** is the right fix to *exempt the built-in* or to *narrow the detector*? Narrowing is usually correct and fixes the class — but check the narrowing does not silently drop a real case (here, "windings differ" would have missed hole/hole double-subtraction, so the rule became "skip only when BOTH are outer"). swept 2026-08-11 **S194**, hits: 1, clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE**
- **JSON-LD ↔ visible-content parity** (**UN-RETIRED 2026-08-08, S190** — retired at S64, re-check ownership moved A2→Pass L at S142, and L's 5th run found it recurred): an `application/ld+json` FAQ/HowTo/ItemList claim that no longer describes the shipped tool. **Detection, as narrowed by S190:** a hit is (a) a **closed enumeration** that asserts the complete set of something the UI has since grown ("one per image, a full sheet, or an existing font file" vs the wizard's four sources), or (b) a description of **how a feature works** that no longer describes it (the dashboard's "assign a preset and an end time to each period" vs the weekly grid). *Merely incomplete prose is still NOT a hit* — that part of S142's convention stands. **Always check the VISIBLE twin**: every claim on these pages has an on-page FAQ counterpart, and S190 found all three twins carrying the identical stale wording in EN and HE, so a JSON-LD-only fix would create a new mismatch. Swept 2026-08-08 (S190), **hits: 3** (font-maker, dashboard, dictionary — all fixed 3237da8), clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. Re-checked in Pass L. Named unswept surface for the next L: `index.html`'s `ItemList` of 8 tools, the 7 `WebApplication` blocks' `featureList` where present, and the generator/flash-cards/torah/trope FAQ+HowTo blocks (S174 hand-verified flash-cards; the rest were checked under the OLD non-enumeration convention and deserve a pass under the narrowed one). **⚑ SWEPT 2026-08-13 (S201, 6th L) — that named surface is now covered, and it held the hits: 2 LIVE mismatches + 7 stale closed enumerations, all one concern** (the 2026-07-23 dictionary theme launch was half-propagated: the CSV FAQ answer + the JSON-LD HowTo step got "theme"; the JSON-LD FAQ answer, the visible HowTo step, the static fallbacks, the meta/og/twitter/WebApp/HowTo descriptions and the index ItemList + card did not — three different states on one answer). **Fixes GATE-2 APPROVED (all 12 sites, wording locked) — queued as S202's first fix; clean streak stays 0 until it ships and re-verifies.** generator/flash-cards/torah/trope claims: CLEAN under the narrowed convention; `featureList` exists on none of the 7 WebApplication blocks. NEW verification rule from this sweep: a parity byte check must cover head copy + visible twins + CSV rows together, and a NEW claim goes into both sides in one commit with identical wording (parity-by-construction — da22334). **⚑ S202 (2026-08-13): both live mismatches CLOSED (0d4373a — all 12 approved sites, insertion-only, pairs re-verified agreeing) — the S190-named unswept surface is now both swept (S201) and fixed (S202), so the next L run starts its clean-streak count from a complete baseline. Streak: 0.**
- **wired-then-clobbered label** (NEW, registered S192): an element that carries a **correct `data-i18n` binding** and is then **overwritten by a JS setter with a hardcoded English literal** — so `applyStaticI18n` translates it and the setter immediately reverts it. Distinct from `pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered` (a builder that runs before `I18n.ready` and is never re-run): here the key IS referenced and the wiring IS correct; a later writer clobbers it. **Why both gates are blind to it:** the literals characteristically sit in a **ternary** (`c ? 'Edit kern class' : 'New kern class'`) or an **array** rather than a plain assignment, which is exactly Check A's documented blind spot; and Check B only measures CSV translation coverage, which is complete. **Detection (repeatable, cheap):** build a map of `id → data-i18n key` from the file's static markup, then scan every `.textContent`/`.innerHTML`/`.value`/`.placeholder` assignment whose RHS contains an English string literal and no `I18n.t(`, resolving the target element by the nearest preceding `getElementById` within ~12 lines. **Triage rule learned on the first run:** that ID-proximity heuristic mismatches — 2 of 4 raw candidates were the generator's printed worksheet subtitles attributed to an unrelated `worksheet.layout.title_aria` binding, and printed output is never-translate anyway, so **always confirm the resolved element really is the bound one before logging**. **Fix shape:** retarget the binding as well as the text (`setAttribute('data-i18n', k)` + `textContent = I18n.t(k)`) — the existing suite idiom at `flash_cards.html` `timerShowLabel`. That is what makes a language switch land, and it avoids the trap of "just add the function to `applyI18n`", which is **illegal** when the function mutates state (`applyI18n` must stay read-only). Guard the text write against the pre-ready case (`t()` returns the key before locales load) so a raw dot-key can never be painted. Swept 2026-08-11 (S192, first sweep): **4 raw / 2 real / 2 fixed / 0 left**, across all 13 root HTML files. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE.
- **theme-flipping-token-on-a-fixed-colour-plate** (NEW, registered S191): a control or text node styled with a colour token that **inverts with the theme** — `var(--text)`, `var(--white)`, `var(--muted)` — placed inside a container painted a **fixed** colour that does not invert (a `--navy`/`--navy-deep` plate, a gold button, a print sheet). One theme composites fine and the other collapses toward 1:1, so a single-theme check clears it and the failure lands in whichever theme the author was not looking at. **This is the documented `--white` pitfall in CLAUDE.md read in the other direction** — that note warns `var(--white)` text disappears on navy in DARK; the S191 hit was `var(--text)` disappearing on navy in **LIGHT** (the bulk dock's `.btn-secondary`, measured 1:1, label rendered as an empty box). **Detection is not greppable** — the token and the plate are in different rules — so it needs the alpha-compositing contrast probe run in **both** themes, with transparent backgrounds walked up to an opaque ancestor. The house fix is the existing navy-plate idiom: literal `#fff` on `rgba(255,255,255,0.12)`. **Consequence-critical (a control whose label is invisible), so it does not retire on a clean streak.** — ACTIVE **2nd sweep 2026-08-11 (S193 Pass A): 1 hit, fixed (cd52e15).** Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **Definition widened: the class has a MIRROR IMAGE** — S191's was an inverting token on a fixed plate; trope_tutor's is a **fixed** colour (the UA default `#0000EE` on an anchor no rule reached) on a plate that **inverts**. Same failure and same 1–2:1 floor, opposite cause; the compositing detector finds both because it measures the result, not the tokens. Detail + the new one-evaluate sub-detector in the sweep log below. **⚑ S194: 1 more hit on a NEW carrier via the S193 UA-default-anchor detector** — the Font Maker's 3 `.hint` anchors (`#0000EE`, **1.81:1** on the dark Advanced panel), fixed 01ebdb5 with the same `--gold-text` one-liner. That makes **3 carriers in 4 sessions** (torah_trainer dock, trope_tutor About, FM hints) and confirms the detector's value: it is one page-evaluate, needs no compositing baseline, and the misses are always anchors the page simply never styled because a `footer a`/`.panel a` rule did not reach them. **Next carriers to sweep: the 5 dormant anchors the S193 run found un-rendered** — enable each one's disclosure state first, which is exactly what turned the FM hint links from "never measured" into a confirmed 1.81:1. **Re-swept 2026-08-12 (S199) via the UA-default-anchor sub-detector, with every disclosure FORCED OPEN so the dormant carriers render: 1 hit (index `.ie-note a`, 1.63:1 dark, fixed 071e326) + 1 REFUTED.** That closes the "5 dormant anchors" queue and the count reconciles exactly: 3 FM hint links (S194) + these 2. **Standing false positive, do not re-chase:** `hebrew_dictionary .skip-results` reads 1.81:1 in dark but only while clipped to 1×1px offscreen; its only visible state is `:focus`, which sets `background:var(--navy); color:#fff`. **A forced-open detector must be paired with a real-state confirmation** — the index hit was re-measured with the gear modal genuinely opened before it was logged. **S202 hit (the mirror image again): the FM wizard modal's `.tbtn.ghost` — a fixed navy-toolbar token (gold-light) on the theme-flipping modal plate — 1.5:1 in light on the gate's only exit; fixed 3a33b1a by rescoping the file's own `.tour-card` repair. Sub-shape family now: inverting-token-on-fixed-plate (S191), UA-anchor-on-inverting-plate (S193/S199), fixed-token-on-inverting-plate (S193 trope, S202 FM).**
- **two-state-ready-flag-for-a-three-state-load** (NEW, registered S189): a lazily-fetched corpus or module whose UI decides what to render from a single truthiness check — `REAL_WORDS.length`, `EMOJI_DATA`, `_wordsReady`. **That flag has two states; the fetch has three** (never attempted / in flight / failed), so the "not ready" arm is also what a FAILED load renders, permanently: either a confident false zero ("0 matching words" — worse than silence, because it is a claim about the user's own selection) or a "Loading…" that never ends, refreshed by every later re-render and language switch. **Detection:** for each lazy fetch, name its ready flag, grep every render that branches on it, and ask what the failure path leaves on screen; then stall the resource AND 502 it headless, reading the end state **after ≥4 s** — a short sample cannot tell "stuck" from "not yet". **Run both languages:** the dictionary instance PASSED in English and FAILED in Hebrew, because the later locale fetch changes whether a re-render lands after the catch. Also check whether the FIRST write happens *before* the fetch exists — the generator's guard was correct and still never fired, because every trigger path painted the caption before calling the loader (fix at the loader, not the call site). Swept 2026-08-08 (S189, Pass F) over the primary slow operation of all 7 tools, **hits: 3 of 7** (generator, flash cards, dictionary — the last with a twin instance two lines away in the same function), **all fixed same-session**; clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **Unswept sibling surfaces named for the next A:** the four transliteration-ESM status lines (`#translitLoadStatus` in generator/flash cards/dictionary, `#optTranslitStatus` in torah), the dashboard's `fetchShabbat`, and torah_trainer's `manifestData`/`parshiyotData` loads. **Re-swept 2026-08-11 (S193 Pass A): CLEAN — all 3 S189 hits fixed, three-state idiom now in place. Clean streak: 1 — ACTIVE.**
- **invalid-SVG-geometry-from-an-unclamped-difference** (NEW, registered S187): an SVG `width`/`height` computed as the difference of two mapped coordinates (`fx(b) - fx(a)`, `x1 - x0`) where one side comes from a **derived** value that can legitimately go negative — so the attribute goes negative, and per spec the browser **does not render the element at all** and logs an error. The overlay silently disappears in exactly the unusual case it was there to explain, and the console error repeats on every render. **Sibling-asymmetry is the tell:** one of a paired accessor clamps (`letterRSB` → `Math.max(0, …)`) and the other forgot to (`letterLSB`). **Detection:** wrap the `innerHTML` setter and regex added markup for `<rect[^>]*(?:width|height)="-` — the raw console count UNDERSTATES the frequency because Chromium collapses repeated identical messages (measured S187: 4 real assignments, 1 console line). Then check every `width="${…}"` built as a difference. **Swept 2026-08-08 (S187) over Hebrew_Font_Maker's 21 rect emitters: 1 hit (`marginGuidesSVG`'s left zone, fixed 74e0530, + the identical `wideFormGuidesSVG` shape guarded), rest clean** — `node-marquee` uses `Math.abs`, `markSelectRect` clamps to MIN 22, the capture-box rect was measured positive, and the handle/point rects are fixed-size. **Not yet swept elsewhere** — the obvious carriers are the generator's worksheet cut-line/QR geometry and torah_trainer's trope-underline print rules. clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **Re-swept 2026-08-11 (S193 Pass A): CLEAN — and both named carriers emit ZERO `<rect>`, so the shape is Font-Maker-only in practice. Clean streak: 1 — ACTIVE.**
- **RTL-inheritance-on-a-Latin-script-container** (NEW, registered S185): a container whose CONTENT is Latin script — an English translation, a transliteration, a Latin-labelled readout — that declares no `direction`, so under the Hebrew UI (`<html dir="rtl">`) it inherits an RTL base direction and every sentence-final period, comma or bracket jumps to the wrong end. Silent in the English UI, silent to `check-i18n` (nothing is untranslated), and it reaches PAPER as well as screen. **Detection:** render each layout in the HE UI and compare `getComputedStyle(el).direction` against the script of the text the element actually carries; assert under `emulateMedia({media:'print'})` too. **⚠ DETECTION AMENDED S187 — the computed-`direction` test alone OVER-REPORTS badly.** Swept over trope_tutor (the tool the S186 pointer named as the unswept carrier): **29 Latin-carrying leaves all compute `direction:rtl`, and per-character `Range` x-position comparison in EN vs HE found 0 of 7 punctuated strings visually reordered** — a single uninterrupted LTR run keeps logical order under an RTL base regardless (bracket pairs included, BD16). So the **mandatory second stage is a visual-order comparison**; a hit requires punctuation that can actually move, which in practice means **sentence-final** punctuation at the END of the run (what the real f5a0d72 hit had), not punctuation enclosed inside it. Counting computed-direction alone would have manufactured 29 false hits here. **Swept 2026-08-06 (S185) over torah_trainer's three reading layouts: 1 hit (side-by-side's two Latin columns, fixed f5a0d72), 2 clean** — interlinear pins `.tt-translit`/`.tt-translation` itself and Page view carries `dir="ltr"` on its bible blocks. **Not yet swept anywhere else** — the obvious carriers are the generator's worksheet/answer-key transliteration cells, flash-cards' card faces, and the dictionary's English/transliteration rows, all of which render Latin content inside a page that flips to `dir="rtl"`. **Pin on the ROW/cell, not the column**: a column-level pin also flips translated `<h3>` chrome, which is a regression (measured S185 — the drafted one-liner in the S181 candidate would have done exactly that). **trope_tutor swept CLEAN 2026-08-08 (S187), screen AND print.** clean streak: 1 — ACTIVE.
- **silent-external-media-failure** (an `<audio>`/`<video>`/media element pointed at a third-party host with play/pause/ended handlers wired but **no `error` handler**, so a blocked or dead origin produces silence while the controls still show a live playing state. Detection: answer the media URL with an error response — `route.abort()` leaves the element STALLED rather than errored, so it never fires — and drive the real play path, because a `preload="none"` element fetches nothing until `play()`. Any control still reading "stop"/"pause" afterwards is a hit.) — **registered + first swept 2026-08-02 (S183)** on `torah_trainer.html` only: **1 hit** (the PocketTorah MP3, fixed a6bf328), clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **Unswept elsewhere:** the trope tutor's `#tuAudio` clip engine plays the same PocketTorah MP3s and has its own `_badFiles`/`onError` path that should be checked against this definition, and the dashboard's YouTube iframe is a third-party media embed with no error surface at all. **Swept 2026-08-08 (S187) over trope_tutor's `#tuAudio` clip engine — the surface the S186 pointer named as the unswept carrier: CLEAN, and MEASURED rather than read.** Per S183's method note the MP3 was fulfilled with a **502** (an `abort` leaves the element stalled, not errored): `MediaError.code 4`, `_badFiles.size 2`, a visible localized `role="status"` line "Audio unavailable right now — check your connection.", 0 pageerror. `playClip` carries a per-load `error` listener **and** a 20 s timeout for the black-holed-network case the pattern names, plus a token guard and seek verification — the reference implementation for this class.
- **print-trailing-dead-space** (padding/margin BELOW the last line of a print flow — page-container bottom padding, a scroll wrapper's, the last block's own margin — which paginates exactly like content, so a document ending near a page boundary pushes empty box onto a sheet of its own. Detection: under `emulateMedia({media:'print'})` at the true printable width, compare the page container's `getBoundingClientRect().bottom` against the deepest bottom edge of any text-bearing descendant; any positive delta is dead space that can cost a sheet. Confirm on real PDFs, never on emulation alone.) — **registered + first swept 2026-08-02 (S182)** on `torah_trainer.html` only: **1 hit** (22–44px depending on layout/paper, fixed 6896ba5), clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **The other 11 root pages have never been checked** — `hebrew_blend_generator.html` and `flash_cards.html` both have heavy print paths and are the obvious next targets.
- **lazily-loaded-dependency-renders-an-empty-shell** (a feature whose data comes from a lazily-loaded external module keeps rendering its full chrome — column, header, row label, legend — when the module never arrives, so the user gets a labelled void with the toggle still reading ON. - **referenced-but-unauthored i18n key** (a key the code LOOKS UP that does not exist in the CSV — the exact inverse of `authored-but-unreferenced`. The user sees the English fallback, so nothing looks broken, but every render logs an `[i18n] missing key` warning, and those warnings are cover for the next real one. **Invisible to BOTH gates: Check A hunts hardcoded literals, Check B checks that CSV keys are translated — neither checks that a referenced key exists**): **registered + swept 2026-07-30 (S172), hits: 28** — `fontmaker.glyphname.<trope>` for all 28 cantillation marks, logged not fixed (needs gate-2 Hebrew). **Detection: load every page in both languages, exercise each panel, and require ZERO `[i18n] missing key` warnings** — the same console sweep as `double-localization`, which is why they should be swept together. **Coverage caveat learned the hard way: the 28 only appear once the trop mark panel is ENABLED, and 15 Pass-B runs never enabled it — a console audit only sees the panels it opens.** Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **S176: the founding instance (28 FM trope names) CLOSED via 42a40a5 — the all-panels-open console sweep now reports 0 missing-key warnings suite-wide. - **sub-floor touch target on a shared small-button class** (NEW, registered S193): a small-control class — `.btn-xs` and its kin — whose size comes from `padding` **alone**, so its rendered height lands below the suite's ratified **30px** floor (WCAG 2.5.8 asks 24px). Because the class is shared, one missing rule puts **every** small button in that tool under the bar at once, which is why this is a class and not a per-button candidate. **Detection (no judgement calls):** headless per page, both themes × desktop/narrow, all panels/drawers open, collect `getBoundingClientRect()` for every `.btn-xs` and count `height < 30` and `height < 24`. **The fix is a copy, not an invention:** flash_cards' S102 declarations — `min-height`/`min-width:30px` plus `display:inline-flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center`, where the inline-flex is what turns the min-height into a taller **box** instead of taller text. **Two guards are mandatory afterwards**, because a min-height reflows tight rows: horizontal page overflow (`scrollWidth - clientWidth`) must not move, and no target may overflow a clipping ancestor. **Swept + fixed 2026-08-11 (S193): 44 rendered targets across 4 files, 44 under 30px, 14 under 24px → all 44 at 30px, overflow unchanged at 0, 0 clipped (97a16b7).** History: S102 set the floor in flash_cards, S178 brought its remainder + the shared folder-tree, S193 closed the other four carriers — **3 sessions, 6 files, same shape**, which is what earns it a registry line. **Remaining surface is everything that is NOT `.btn-xs`** (torah_trainer's candidate still names ~50 such controls). Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **⚑ S194 WIDENS THE CLASS PAST `.btn-xs`:** torah_trainer's `.slider-row .sreset` (5 buttons, **24×21**) is the same shape under a different class name — size from `padding` alone, no `min-height`/`min-width` — so the pattern is *any* small-control class sized by padding, not the `.btn-xs` family specifically. Fixed 0164489 with the identical declarations `.btn-xs` already carries in that same file. **Detection lesson, and it is the important half:** a naive `min(w,h) < 30` census over `button,a,input,select,[role=button]` **overcounts badly** — it returned 59 (S191) then 31 (S194) for the same page, and the majority were **0×0 visually-hidden checkboxes** whose styled label is the real hit area and **4px range-slider tracks** whose thumb is draggable. Note the shared-runtime case needed its own iteration precisely because one rule there moves every page at once — verify suite-wide (S196 used 24 measurement cells + a 36-cell reflow guard at 1280/800/420). **RE-OPENED 2026-08-13 (S200) — the S196 "census CLOSED, 0 known members left" note was true only of `.btn-xs`.** hebrew_dictionary's `.pos-all-btn` is a second shared small-button class of the same shape (height from `padding` alone), and it rendered 10 of its 12 buttons at 20–22px: **the first instance found under WCAG 2.5.8's 24px, not merely under the house 30px floor** (f4b5751, 40/48 → 0/48 sub-floor). **Definition widened accordingly: the class is "a shared small-control class sized by padding alone", NOT "`.btn-xs`" — detect by measuring every small control's rendered height, not by grepping one class name.** The mandatory guards held again (page overflow unchanged, no clipping-ancestor overflow) and a third is now recommended: confirm the taller box does not clip its own label, **in Hebrew as well as English**, since Hebrew labels are the longer ones. **S201 adds two more carriers** (both queued by the S178 haul, both shipped): hebrew_dictionary `.cb-label` — 57 rows, 39 under 24px at 19.2px, height from `line-height` alone (the class's first non-button member) → `min-height:24px` + 16×16 input (387a6a1); and the `.font-size-row input[type=range]` twins in dictionary + generator — **a new sub-shape: an explicit `height:4px` shrinks the OPERABLE box while the native thumb overflows it and looks grabbable** — 6 sliders → 24px with the visual provably unchanged via element screenshots (3ae6891). Original definition: (a store the tool's `IVRIT_CFG.apply()` is fully able to restore, but `gather()` never writes — so the tool's own `.ivrit` backup silently omits it, while the index.html AllTools bundle carries it correctly): **registered + swept 2026-07-25 (S159, Pass G), hits: 2 of the 3 `.ivrit` tools.** (1) **flash_cards — student profiles, P1 data-loss (FIXED d7a6f23):** `apply()` reads `data.flashCardProfiles` and calls `mergeProfilesBlob`, with a comment saying it restores profiles "when the file includes them" — `gather()` never included them, so the file carried `profileFolders` **with none of the profiles inside**. Restoring on a new machine returned empty folders and lost every student's drill history. (2) **generator — `hebFont` + `livePreview`, P3 (FIXED 3a06669):** `getSettings()` carried `hebFontSize` but not the font itself, so a restored worksheet used the right size in the default face. (3) **dashboard — CLEAN** (its settings blob is exactly what `liveState` already carries). clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (backup completeness / data-loss-on-new-machine, the same carve-out as localStorage-vs-AllTools). **Why the existing sweep missed it:** localStorage-vs-AllTools checks keys against **index.html's three functions**, and both gaps were correctly registered *there* — the per-tool `.ivrit` payload is a second, independent registry that nothing was checking. **Detection (cheap, headless):** for each page with an `IVRIT_CFG`, call `IVRIT_CFG.gather()` live and diff `Object.keys(result)` + `Object.keys(result.liveState)` against the keys that tool owns in the AllTools table; anything owned-but-absent is a candidate. Then confirm by round-trip — seed real state, export, wipe, `IVRIT_CFG.apply(parsed.data, 'merge')`, and assert the state came back. **Read `apply()` first:** in both hits `apply()` was already correct, so the whole defect was one missing line in `gather()`. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168), hits: 0** — all four `IVRIT_CFG` carriers checked; `apply()` and `gather()` are aligned, and the apparent gaps are the documented AllTools-bundle **alias** keys (`data.presets || data.flashCardPresets`). Generator: `liveState` = 140 keys and NOW includes `hebFont` + `livePreview` (both S159 hits verified FIXED). NEW: (1) **flash_cards `flashBackupSave()`** — a scope-aware save path that shadows the shared engine's `ivritSaveFile()` and hand-builds its payload, omitting BOTH folder trees in all three scopes while `gather()` (unused by the button) includes them and `apply()` restores them; (2) **index.html `mergeFlashCardProfiles`** — the existing-name branch merges `results` only and never reads `src.ladder`, silently dropping a student's Learning Path progress on import (its flash_cards sibling `mergeProfilesBlob` has the extra `mergeLadder` pass). Also logged P3: `pbStreak` is in AllTools but in no arm of the FlashCards `.ivrit`. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE, consequence-critical.
- **symbol-only accessible name** (an icon-only control whose ENTIRE accessible name is a glyph with no letter or digit — "×", "↺", "✕", "⬛", an emoji run — because for `button`/`a`/`role=button` the ACCNAME chain takes **name-from-content BEFORE `title`**, so a correct translated `title` on the very same element is never reached. A screen reader announces "multiplication sign, button" or, for an unmapped glyph, nothing): **registered + swept suite-wide 2026-07-29 (S167, Pass C), hits: 50 across 6 tools** — dashboard 27, font-maker 13, generator 4, flash-cards 5, dictionary 3, torah 2; trope_tutor, index, resources, contact, privacy and 404 **clean**. **All 50 fixed** (ddc9180 + 2c4b78f): 38 were pure wiring (mirror the existing translated `title` into `aria-label`), 12 had no label at all and took 9 new keys + 3 reuses. clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (a11y blocker, and it re-appears every time someone adds an icon button). **Detection:** compute the ACCNAME chain in-page over the focusable set and flag `name && !/\p{L}|\p{N}/u.test(name)` — but run it BOTH ways, because each method misses what the other catches: the runtime sweep missed the generator's two collapsed-section resets and every overlay-only button, while a static `<button ...>INNER</button>` scan missed all 4 dashboard sites built by the `mk()` helper and needed the modals driven open to be believed. **Two exemptions, deliberate:** a `div[role="tab"]`/`role="button"` whose content is real text IS named (name-from-content) — a hand-rolled shim that only allows content for `button`/`a` reports these as nameless, which is how S167 briefly "found" a nonexistent Font-Maker defect; and a glyph label that is genuinely decorative beside visible text is fine. **Fix rule:** when mirroring inside a JS builder, gate it on the label having no letters, so a worded button keeps its visible text as its name (WCAG 2.5.3 Label in Name). **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): 2 hits, logged not fixed** — the My Fonts row buttons in BOTH carriers (`renderMyFontsList` in the Font Maker, `renderMyFontsManager`'s `mk()` helper in index.html): `⬇`/`🗑` textContent with a correctly-translated `title` that name-from-content never reaches. Both methods run as required (static parse of 909 `<button>`/`<a href>` elements across 10 files + runtime ACCNAME over driven-open overlays). Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE, consequence-critical.
- nameless-adjacent-text-labeled control (a visible interactive control — toggle switch, slider, number field, select, colour well — whose only label is **adjacent text** that is never programmatically associated, so it has NO accessible name; the wrapping `<label class="toggle"/"switch">` holds just the input + its slider span, and non-checkbox inputs have no `<label for>`): **re-swept 2026-07-25 (S158, Pass A): 0 new hits; the 4 residual FIXED (74c2fcd) → the suite is now at 0 nameless interactive controls.** clean streak: **1** — KEPT ACTIVE (consequence-critical). **Detector caveat learned S158:** force-unhiding `.hide`/`display:none` can expose a control that is *only* nameless while hidden — Font Maker's `#nodeEditToggle` is built empty and labelled by `updateNodeEditToggle()` in the same synchronous call that reveals it, so it is named whenever visible; treat a hidden-only hit as a false positive after checking the reveal path. Original sweep: **registered + swept 2026-07-25 (S157, Pass C), hits: 122 across the whole suite** — generator 90, flash_cards 31, dictionary 1; dashboard / torah / trope / index / resources already at **0** from the S135 / S146 / earlier C passes. **118 fixed** (1cd1362, c91a121, fdce7c9); the 4 residual flash_cards min/max spinbuttons are blocked on a gate-2 copy decision, not on code. clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (a11y blocker: a screen-reader user hears "checkbox" with nothing to say WHICH option, on the tools' primary settings surfaces). **Detection (re-usable, and the important lesson):** read the **real Chromium AX tree** via CDP `Accessibility.enable` + `Accessibility.getFullAXTree`, filter to interactive roles (`checkbox`/`slider`/`spinbutton`/`combobox`/`ColorWell`/`textbox`), and flag `!node.name.value`. A hand-rolled JS name shim **over-reports badly** (S157's first pass claimed 45 false positives — `role="button"` divs like the letter tiles ARE named from content, which only the real AX algorithm knows). **Two traps worth remembering:** (1) **hidden surface hides hits** — the generator showed 8 nameless until collapsed panels were expanded AND `display:none` sidebar rows unhidden, then 90; flash_cards showed 14 until `.hide` blocks were force-opened, then 31. Always force the full control surface open before counting. (2) **non-empty ≠ correct** — verify each computed name: both the generator's and flash_cards' TTS sliders picked up **"Slow"**, the range's low-end caption, and a naive row-scoped `aria-labelledby` can swallow a tooltip's whole body into the name. Check for names >48 chars and eyeball the list. **RE-OPENED 2026-07-27 (S164): NOT clean in `Hebrew_Font_Maker.html`.** The S157 sweep and S125's `vSlider()` pass both missed sliders built **by hand** rather than through the helper: **20 nameless `input[type=range]`** in the mark, precomposed-form and anchor panels. **13 fixed (18067f0)** — those whose visible localized label sits on the slider's own line, so the name reuses that exact `I18n.t()` call; **7 remain** (label lives elsewhere, one still hardcoded English) and are logged as a candidate. **Detection lesson:** auditing rendered sliders alone under-counts badly — the owning panels only render with specific state (a selected mark, a precomp target, the anchor stage), so an initial headless sweep saw 12 sliders and reported 1 nameless when the static truth was 20. Sweep the source AND drive each owning render function. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **RE-SWEPT + CLOSED 2026-07-29 (S166):** the 7 residual Font Maker sliders are named (3219909), so a static scan of all 35 `input[type=range]` in that file finds **0 without `aria-label`/`aria-labelledby`/`title`**, and the real AX tree shows **0 nameless of 17 rendered** across EN/HE × light/dark × 1280/800. **The suite is back to 0 nameless interactive controls.** clean streak: **1**. **Detector lessons added (all three produced a false clean before being caught):** panel builders in Font Maker take **arguments** (`renderTraceLeft(L, l)`, `renderWideFormControls(pc)`) — calling them bare throws into a `catch` and the sweep silently reports the handful of always-rendered sliders as the whole population; `project.letters` is an **Array** of objects carrying `.codepoint`, not a codepoint-keyed map; and **top-level `let` globals cannot be assigned from `page.evaluate`** (the assignment lands on `window`, which the lexical binding shadows) — drive state through `_selectItem`/`_setWorkMode` instead. Corollary to the standing rule: **a sweep that reports "0 hits" must also report how many controls it actually saw.** **S178: 14 dictionary controls hit and FIXED same-session (13 color pickers + ttsRateSlider, 2582737). ACTIVE.** **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): 1 hit, logged** — the Font Maker's two kern-pair side `<select>`s (`renderKerningSection`'s `sideUI`), whose sibling `<input>` one line above IS aria-labelled from the same `sideLabel`. Surface: every select/non-button input/textarea/contenteditable/role=switch on 8 pages × 2 languages × 2 theme-viewport combos, plus driven states (11 FM category tabs with marks enabled, etc.). Clean streak: 0 (was 1) — KEPT ACTIVE.
- dead-feature-guard (a `typeof X === 'function'` / feature-detection guard whose **preferred** implementation does not exist on that page, so the guarded branch can never run and control silently falls through to a worse path — or to nothing): **re-swept 2026-07-25 (S158, Pass A): 0 NEW hits** (23 distinct guards across 16 files; only the registered-benign generator `saveSettings` remains, and S156's fix incidentally REMOVED the `renderCustomWordlist` guard — so 3 of the original 4 are now resolved). clean streak: **1**. **Detector caveat learned S158:** a `typeof X === 'function'` check on a **function parameter** (`js/i18n.js` `onChange(fn)`) is a false positive — the declaration regex looks for `function X(`/`const X =`/`X =`, which a parameter never matches. Skip names bound as a parameter of the enclosing function. Original sweep: **registered + swept 2026-07-24 (S155, Pass F), hits: 4 of 25 non-external guards.** (1) dictionary `showAppToast` → always `alert()`ed — **FIXED 0f60983**; (2) generator `renderRealWords` → translit restyle was a **no-op on Real Words worksheets** — real **P2**, **FIXED S156 (9e95399)** after the S155 attempt was reverted; the fix also had to clear a second, non-guard defect (a stale shallow-copy `rwRenderCache`), so a dead guard can mask more than one bug; (3) generator `renderCustomWordlist` → doubly dead (its `cwModeActive` condition is a documented retired flag, always false) — harmless; (4) generator `saveSettings` in `setVowelScheme` → dead but harmless (the generator has no settings-blob autosave; `vowelColorScheme` persists via `getSettings()` → presets/`.ivrit`). clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE** (S156: 2 of the 4 registered hits now fixed; the 2 remaining are the documented-benign ones) (a dead guard is invisible: no error, no console warning, and the code *reads* as if the good path runs). **Detection:** regex every `typeof (window.)?NAME === 'function'`, drop an allowlist of genuinely-external globals (browser APIs + CDN libs), then check whether NAME is declared/assigned anywhere in that file. **Known false positive:** a guard on a **function parameter** (`js/i18n.js` `onChange(fn)`) — correct validation, not a dead branch; exclude params before reporting. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168): 1 HIT of 27 guards inspected** — the generator's `setVowelScheme` guarded a `saveSettings()` the file defines zero times (a copy of flash cards', where it exists). **Fixed 766f723** by replacing the line with a comment rather than deleting it silently, since the dead guard misled in both directions. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): 0 NEW hits, clean streak 2.** 33 `typeof … === 'function'` guards enumerated across all 12 root pages + i18n-test + `js/i18n.js` + `pwa.js` + `sw.js`, each resolved against same-file declarations AND confirmed at runtime in page scope via `eval('typeof '+name)` (so global `let`/`const` bindings are seen, not just `window` props) over 7 loaded pages, 0 pageerrors each. The S158 parameter-false-positive caveat still holds.
- **modal-focus-trap** (an element declaring `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal="true"` — which tells assistive tech the rest of the page is inert — with **no Tab/Shift+Tab wrap**, so keyboard focus walks out behind the dialog onto controls the AT has been told do not exist; worst on a full-viewport overlay, where the escaped focus ring is not visible anywhere): **registered + swept suite-wide 2026-07-26 (S161, Pass I), 2 hits of 9 dialogs, both fixed (ec41bb0).** Detection: enumerate every `aria-modal="true"` container, open each **via its real opener** (forcing `.open`/`display` skips the wiring and gives a false result — and an unpopulated dialog under-reports its focusables), focus the first focusable inside, then press Tab `n+6` times and Shift+Tab the same, counting frames where `document.activeElement.closest(container)` is null. **Hits: `#frModal` (dashboard first-run card) and `#pickOverlay` (dashboard student picker).** Clean: dashboard settings drawer, dictionary word modal, torah + trope settings drawers, index gear modal, Font Maker (global `_activeModal` trap covers its 14 dialogs). Clean streak: **1**. **KEPT ACTIVE regardless of streak is NOT claimed** — this is accessibility-scoped, not security/data-loss, so it auto-retires at 3 clean sweeps per the rule. **Note for the next sweep:** a dialog that only appears for a brand-new user (first-run cards) is invisible to Pass C, which always runs with populated storage — sweep this pattern from a **fresh context**. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168): CLEAN at runtime.** Static counting cannot decide this class (a trap may be written `e.key !== 'Tab'`, or live in a shared helper), so it was measured: the dashboard settings drawer, torah settings drawer and trope settings drawer all hold focus through 30 tabs, and the dashboard **student picker** holds it through 6 when tested in isolation. **Two false alarms on the way, both mine:** filtering candidates on `offsetParent !== null` finds nothing at all, because **`offsetParent` is `null` for `position: fixed`** — which every one of these drawers is; and probing `[aria-modal=true]` with `.pop()` can latch onto a *second* visible dialog (the first-run `frModal`) and report the wrong element leaking. Test one dialog per page load. **S178 (dictionary): CLEAN — both aria-modal dialogs trap and restore focus, verified by tab-cycling receipts (first full-tool clean pass).** **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): CLEAN, clean streak 2** — all `aria-modal="true"` containers enumerated (14 static in the Font Maker, 3 static + 1 JS-set in the dashboard, 2 JS-set in flash_cards incl. `fcModalize`, which now serves the NEW ladder map / weakness view / profile chooser, plus `ivritAskMode`, and 1 each in generator/dictionary/index/torah/trope), each opened via its REAL opener and Tab/Shift+Tab-walked. The S161 fixes (`#frModal`, `#pickOverlay`) held. Auto-retires at 3 clean (a11y-scoped, no carve-out).
- pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered — **4th instance fixed 2026-08-06 (S185)**: torah_trainer's TTS voice readout, which `applyI18n` had never re-run (30fe852). **Fix-shape note for the next instance: adding the function to `applyI18n` is only half the fix.** If the pre-ready writer also REGISTERS a listener on a target that survives re-render (here `speechSynthesis`), re-running it from `applyI18n` stacks one listener per language switch — hoist the handler out of the closure and give it a name so registration is idempotent. Verified by counting: 5× applyI18n → 0 added listeners. Original definition: (an `I18n.t()` call during init — before `I18n.ready` resolves — writing text or an attribute onto a node that the page's `applyI18n` never re-renders, so the returned **raw key string** sticks permanently, in **both** languages): **re-swept 2026-07-25 (S158, Pass A): 19 NEW hits, all fixed (790ca80)** — torah_trainer's 13 vowel + 6 trope colour inputs, whose `aria-label` came from `I18n.t()` inside `initColorPickers`/`initTropeColorPickers` at init while `applyI18n` rebuilt six *other* things but not those two lists. **Hebrew-only this time** (English resolves pre-ready), which is why the S152 sweep — and every pass since — missed it: an EN-only raw-key scan cannot see this. **Sweep both languages.** clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE, consequence-critical. **Fix shape to reuse:** when a builder calls `I18n.t()`, either re-call that builder from `applyI18n` (correct when the builder is a pure re-render, as here) or use flash_cards' English-fallback + `data-i18n-aria-label` pattern — but the latter cannot carry `{param}` substitution, so a parameterised name forces the re-call. Original: **registered + swept 2026-07-24 (S152, Pass B), hits: 44** — 42 tooltip triggers whose `aria-label` was the literal `shared.tip.aria_more_info` (generator 31 / dictionary 5 / torah 5 / trope 1) + 2 visible counters (`#pickerRosterCount` showing `dashboard.picker.roster_count.other`, `#exportCount` showing `dictionary.export.count.other`). All 44 fixed (02693b6, caaa0fc); **flash_cards already carried the correct pattern** (English fallback + `data-i18n-aria-label`, translated by the on-ready `applyStaticI18n` sweep) and is the canonical reference. clean streak: 0 — **ACTIVE, consequence-critical** (a11y-blocking + user-visible wrong text, AND invisible to both existing gates: the runtime's `dictLoaded` warn-gate deliberately suppresses the `[i18n] miss` console warning pre-ready — see the comment in `js/i18n.js` — and `check-i18n` is static-only, so it cannot see a runtime timing bug). **Detection (re-usable):** headless raw-key scan over all 12 pages × EN/HE — body text nodes (excluding `SCRIPT`/`STYLE`/`TEMPLATE`) plus `title`/`aria-label`/`placeholder`/`alt` — matching a page-prefix key regex (`shared|index|generator|dashboard|flashcards|dictionary|torah|trope|fontmaker|resources|contact|privacy|notfound|pwa` + `.`-segments); post-fix scan = **0** suite-wide. **3rd instance 2026-07-27 (S165, Pass F): torah_trainer's failure path.** The empty-reading card and the status line are written before `I18n.ready` whenever a data fetch fails fast; `applyI18n` re-rendered the reading **only when one was loaded**, so the empty branch kept its raw keys in both languages. Fixed in place (never via `renderEmptyReading()` — that advances a running tour). **New sub-lesson:** healing a stuck key from the *visible text* is not enough — the line then stops following language switches; record the key at write time (`setStatus(msg, isError, key)`) and re-resolve from it. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): 1 NEW hit (3rd instance), logged** — torah `#optTranslitStatus`, written by `wireTranslit()`'s two listeners and healed by nothing, so a slow/failed locale load freezes it at the literal key in BOTH languages. Scan covered text nodes + 10 attributes, visible AND hidden, in both languages, at rest / after driving / after a live switch. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE, consequence-critical. **5th instance fixed 2026-08-07 (S186, 62a9981): the S179 hit itself — both #optTranslitStatus writers record dataset.i18nKey and the existing applyI18n healer re-resolves; read-only heal, no new listeners, so the S185 listener-leak trap does not arise. 0 known open instances.**

- _**Retirement rule (as applied):** a pattern retires after 3 consecutive clean sweeps **UNLESS it is consequence-critical** (security or data-loss). The three that hit 3-clean at S64 — unescaped-input/unsafe-parse (XSS), localStorage-vs-AllTools (backup), destructive-bulk (teacher data) — are **deliberately kept ACTIVE**: new code reintroduces them anytime and they re-sweep cheaply, so the safety margin outweighs the noise. Only structural/cosmetic classes auto-retire. Retired patterns are re-checked only in pass A2._

- **placeholder-as-only-accessible-name** (a text input/textarea whose ONLY name source is its `placeholder` — no `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, `label[for]`, wrapping `<label>` or `title`. Under ACCNAME `placeholder` is the last-resort source, so the name is announced on an empty field and then **vanishes once the field holds a value** — i.e. exactly while the teacher is using it. **Detection:** headless, per page, over `input:not([type=hidden]):not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]):not([type=button]):not([type=submit]), textarea` that carry a `placeholder`; walk the ACCNAME precedence chain in-page and count anything landing on `PLACEHOLDER-ONLY`. A static grep for `<input …placeholder…>` without `aria-label` **over-reports** — it can't see `label[for]` or a wrapping `<label>`): **registered + swept 2026-07-26 (S163), hits: 17 across 6 tools, ALL FIXED (4451a41).** By tool — dashboard 6, flash_cards 4, generator 2, dictionary 2, font-maker 2, index 1; **clean:** torah_trainer, trope_tutor, resources, contact, privacy (all already named via `aria-label`/`label[for]`/wrapping label). clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. Consequence: accessibility blocker, cheap to reintroduce (every new text input is a chance), so keep ACTIVE. **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): 3 hits, logged** — flash_cards `#newProfileNameInput` (focused on load, so it is the first stop for a new visitor arriving via the delta's `?path=1` deep link) and `#acCustom` (announces as spinbutton "#"), plus FM `#saveAsNameInput` (PARTIAL: pre-filled, so its accessible name is text no user ever sees). Verified through Chromium CDP `Accessibility.getPartialAXTree`, not a hand-rolled chain. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **S186: all 3 hits FIXED (c566f50) — aria-label reusing existing translated strings, AX-verified EN+HE; 0 known open sites.**
- **class-only-selected-state** (a mutually-exclusive selector button that marks the current choice with a CSS `.active` class and **no `aria-pressed`/`aria-checked`**, so assistive tech announces every sibling identically and the user cannot tell which option is live. **Detection:** grep `?'active':''` template toggles **plus** runtime `classList.toggle('active', …)` setters, then confirm headless that every rendered `.mode-btn`/`.mark-sq`/`.count-btn` agrees `class .active ⟺ aria-pressed="true"`. **Two traps:** (a) the same button class is used for plain **action** buttons in FM — `aria-pressed` on those would be wrong, so judge per control, not per class; (b) buttons inside an **unopened modal** carry pre-sync markup and must be excluded — judging them manufactures phantom hits): **registered + swept 2026-07-26 (S163, Pass H), hits: 20, ALL FIXED** — 19 in `Hebrew_Font_Maker.html` (03340be: 15 template-literal + 4 static custom-glyph toggles) + 1 in `flash_cards.html` (07bebf7: the ten deck-size buttons). **Swept clean:** generator, dashboard, dictionary, torah_trainer, trope_tutor, index, resources (0 `?'active':''` toggles; 6–28 `aria-pressed` uses each). clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **RE-SWEPT 2026-07-29 (S167, Pass C): 2 live groups found, both logged rather than fixed — the variety governor blocked them** (flash_cards was at its 2-iteration cap when the finding landed). (a) `.weak-tab` ×4 in the flash_cards Weaknesses view: `aria-pressed` measured `null` on all four in every matrix run, so AT announces the four tabs identically; the fix is one line in `paintTabs()`, which already loops them to repaint. (b) ✅ **DONE S168, 83ed5c8** — `#ivritModeAuto`/`#ivritModeManual` in the shared `.ivrit` engine; it turned out `index.html` already carried the correct wiring and the other three carriers had drifted behind it, so the fix was to propagate the known-good copy (all four byte-identical again, sha 1755507c). clean streak: **0 — ACTIVE**. **Detection note:** grep for `.active`/`.selected` toggling is not enough — assert `aria-pressed`/`aria-checked`/`aria-selected` is non-null at runtime on each group, since the state is often painted by a JS repaint function rather than markup. **Re-swept 2026-07-29 (S168): 1 group FIXED (83ed5c8), 2 still open.** Fixed: the shared `.ivrit` Automatic/Manual buttons — and the diagnosis matters, because it was **drift, not omission**: `index.html` already had the correct `aria-pressed` wiring and the other three carriers had fallen behind, so the fix was to propagate the known-good copy and restore byte-identity (sha 1755507c). Still open: `.weak-tab` ×4 (flash_cards), and — **new, found this session** — the **Default/TaL AM vowel-scheme buttons in all four scheme tools**, which is now the top S169 candidate. clean streak: **0** — ACTIVE. **S169: both remaining groups FIXED — the class is now at 0 open groups.** The vowel-scheme buttons (2322027, three tools not four — see the Done entry; flash_cards was already covered by its generic `initPillAria()`) and `.weak-tab` ×4 (b98ada6, fixed in `paintTabs()`). clean streak: **0 → next A sweep can start counting.** **Detection lesson added:** when a group looks broken across N carriers, run the negative control per carrier — S169's split (6 of 8 failing, with flash_cards passing both before and after) is the fingerprint of "already covered by a different mechanism", and patching it anyway would have added a duplicate that diverges from that file's idiom. **S178 (dictionary, Pass C): 4 NEW hits — bulk-selected cards + 3 segmented groups (mode/gender/color-mode), logged as 2 candidates. ACTIVE, streak reset.** **Re-swept 2026-08-01 (S179, Pass A): 2 NEW groups, logged** — flash_cards' Presets/Profiles/Both backup scope (no `pressed` property at all, while the visually identical `.ivrit-mode-btn` pair in the same panel DOES expose it) and the dashboard's 8-option `#emojiSetBtns`. Both traps respected: judged per control, and restricted to VISIBLE elements so unopened-modal pre-sync markup could not manufacture phantom hits. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE. **Swept + fixed 2026-08-06 (S184): the 3 dictionary groups closed (32be832) — `#modeSwitcher`, emoji gender, colour-coding mode — all nine buttons seeded with `aria-pressed` in markup and synced through one `setSegActive()` helper. NEW SUB-SHAPE FOUND: a group whose sync selector is not scoped to its own group. `.emoji-gender-btn` is worn by BOTH the gender trio and the Words/Emojis/Shoresh mode switcher, and two of the three sync sites swept it unscoped, so choosing a gender left the mode switcher with no highlighted option at all (proved against the pre-fix file). When fixing this class elsewhere, check that each group's selector cannot match a sibling group. The flash-cards `setBackupScope` + dashboard `#emojiSetBtns` arms (S179) remain OPEN.** **S186: both S179 arms FIXED (f3800c8) — aria-pressed seeded in markup + synced at every toggle site; the class is again at 0 KNOWN open groups. Clean streak: 0 — ACTIVE (the next A sweep over the S179–S186 delta can start counting).**

- falsy-zero (`s.field || default` silently discarding a stored `0`/`''`/`false` in a numeric/boolean restore) | retired 2026-07-17 (S93) | 3 consecutive clean sweeps: 1 hit S64 (FM `spec.version||1.0`, b9c1aa3), then clean S73, S83 (FM v4.18→v4.26 slider/geometry guards), S93 (dashboard movable-panels — array-order `panelLayout`, no numeric restores). ## Recurring-pattern sweep status
- **A (S203, 2026-08-13): 9 classes over `2771b39..HEAD` (56 commits, +441 — the S194–S202 fixes + the outside-loop v5.12 feature), 8 clean / 1 hit, hit fixed in-session (1410e7b).** Method: delta-diff signature greps over added lines (setItem / HTML sinks / parse+assign / bulk loops / fetch / transition+animation / `<rect>` emitters / new controls / `${…}` interpolations), each hit attributed and grounded — receipts in the S203 Pass A Done entry. Highlights: the consequence-critical trio hits streak 13 with every added sink individually attributed; the delta's one new blob-read (`applyPanelStates` on `panelsCollapsed`) is the class's reference consult shape; the one new `<rect>` emitter is negative-proof by construction; all 6 added motion lines are S199's own neutralizers; the post-S201 SEO byte-delta is exactly the approved theme haul; both S201/S202 pattern-class fixes re-verified holding. **HIT: sub-floor touch target on the FM contours rows** (~19–21px, a `.btn-xs`-census blind spot). **Grounding also killed two queue entries before any fix was spent on them:** torah `#settingsBackdrop` and torah dark selected-verse were already fixed (915526d S198, a01087b S199) — their unchecked candidate boxes were stale markers, corrected this session.
- **error-status-clobbered-by-a-later-routine-write (NEW, S199):** FIRST sweep 2026-08-12 (S199 Pass B), narrow and receipted. Surface: the **3 tools that fetch a `data/` corpus at load** (torah_trainer, trope_tutor, hebrew_dictionary); the other 4 request none, so they cannot carry the class. Method: block `data/` at the route with `serviceWorkers:'block'`, let init finish, then read each tool's own live region. **1 hit / 2 clean** — torah_trainer showed "Pick a parsha to start." where its catch had set the data-load diagnostic (fixed b5bd9c0); trope_tutor correctly holds "Example clips unavailable (couldn’t load the trope index)…" plus a matching `#drillStartNote`, and the dictionary correctly holds "Couldn't load the word list…". Broader census recorded but NOT swept: 25 `setStatus` writers in torah_trainer and 12 in trope_tutor — the neutral `setStatus('')` clears all sit on success paths. Next sweep should widen to non-init failure paths (a mid-session fetch failure followed by a routine re-render).
- **animation-outside-its-reduced-motion-block (NEW, S198):** FIRST sweep 2026-08-12 (S198), and deliberately a **narrow** one — the census covered the `.settings-backdrop`/`.settings-modal` drawer shape across its **3 carriers** (`classroom_dashboard`, `torah_trainer`, `trope_tutor`), not every animated selector on every page. Method: locate each carrier's backdrop rule, extract its `transition:` declaration, then test whether the selector appears inside that file's `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block. **Result: 1 hit** — torah_trainer declared `transition:opacity 0.25s` and neutralized only its `.settings-modal` sibling (fixed 915526d, grouping the pair on one line like the dashboard does). The other two carriers were already correct, and **that is what downgraded the finding from a class to an instance before it was written up.** Runtime confirmation used a **control arm** (`reducedMotion:'reduce'` vs `'no-preference'`, 12 cells): `0s`/`0s` under reduce, `0.25s` under no-preference — without the second state the probe proves nothing. **Owed next A run:** the general selector-subtraction sweep across all 11 animation-bearing pages, which this narrow census does **not** discharge.
- **content-dependent-tour-step-miscounts-the-tour:** FIRST sweep 2026-08-12 (S197 Pass I). Surface: all 7 tour-bearing tools, current-state (the class had never been looked for, so a delta sweep would have under-covered it). Method: drive the real tour (`tourStart()` + the real Next control) on a fresh profile, record the counter text at each step, compare shown-vs-declared — then repeat with that tool's own `data/` route-aborted and `serviceWorkers:'block'` (without the block a live worker serves `data/` from `DATA_CACHE` and the abort never bites — S165). **2 carriers, both fixed in 27554b4; trope_tutor is the negative control (6/6 with data blocked); generator/dashboard/flash-cards/font-maker target static chrome only.** ⚑ The pass's own FIRST probe produced a false positive here worth remembering: a static per-step loop that calls every `reveal()` in sequence reported the dashboard's `#movePanelsBtn` step as unresolvable, because each later `reveal()` collapses the panel an earlier one opened. **The real walk showed 8/8 and the walk is the authoritative arm** — resolve steps the way the engine does, one at a time, in order.
- **wired-then-clobbered label (NEW, S192):** FIRST sweep 2026-08-11 (S192 Pass K). Surface: all 13 root HTML files, current-state detection (not delta-scoped — the class had never been looked for, so a delta sweep would have under-covered it). Method as recorded in Pattern health: `id → data-i18n` map from static markup, cross-referenced against every English-literal assignment to `textContent`/`innerHTML`/`value`/`placeholder`. **4 raw candidates → 2 real, both fixed** (`Hebrew_Font_Maker.html` `#kernClassTitle` 3491258; `classroom_dashboard.html` `#groupNLabel` 345859b) **→ 0 remaining**. **2 dismissed with cause:** `hebrew_blend_generator.html`'s two `ws-subtitle` writes are printed worksheet output (CLAUDE.md never-translate) AND the heuristic had mis-resolved them to an unrelated `#wsTitle` aria binding — a double reason to dismiss, and the reason the triage rule is now written into the pattern definition. Both real hits had their exact counterpart key **already authored and already translated**, which is what makes this class so cheap to fix and so invisible without a runtime check.
- **authored-but-unreferenced i18n key family:** **re-swept 2026-08-11 (S192 Pass K) with a NEW and far better method — 35 hits, 8 real, 2 fixed.** The static census this pattern was originally defined around has now failed **three times** (S166: 721/2/424 across three detectors; S192: 1082, then 677 after generalizing wildcard expansion to catch prefixes assigned to a variable first, e.g. `const k = 'fontmaker.glyphname.' + slug` — still swamped by dynamically-keyed families). **Superseding method, use this from now on:** load each of the 12 pages with the Hebrew UI (`hebrewBlender_lang='he'`, local HTTP, `serviceWorkers:'block'`), expand every progressive-disclosure surface, collect all English still on screen (text nodes + `title`/`aria-label`/`placeholder`), and **reverse-look-up each string against `en.json`, keeping only matches whose `he.json` value is different**. It measures the user-visible symptom rather than reference reachability, so dynamic keys cost nothing, and each hit arrives with its candidate key attached. **It needs human triage on exactly one axis — coincidental cross-concept string matches** (the generator's `Letter` is the *paper size* not the colour mode; the dashboard's `Default` is a *user preset name*; `Alef` ×6 is a *font family* in the picker, not the glyph) — plus the standing CLAUDE.md scope boundary (the trope tutor's Mercha/Tipcha/Munach/Etnachta are the taught pronunciation names, never-translate). Fixed this session: the footer nav links on 4 chrome pages (3d342f5) and the flash-cards vowel pills (3dc4f41). Logged: the dictionary's 9 vowel sub-labels, torah_trainer's duplicated drawer nav block, and (as a gated mapping decision, not wiring) the dictionary POS badge.
- JSON-LD ↔ visible-content parity: **swept 2026-08-13 (S201 Pass L, 6th run) — 2 LIVE mismatches + 7 stale closed enumerations, all on the surface S190 named unswept (the dictionary theme-copy haul; gate-2 approved, ships S202).** Byte check extended to the visible twins + CSV (0 changes since S190, so S190's verifications carried forward); 34/34 blocks parse. Prior: **swept 2026-08-08 (S190 Pass L, 5th run) — 3 HITS, pattern UN-RETIRED.** Surface: all 35 `ld+json` blocks on the 13 root pages, parsed by script, with the FAQ/HowTo claims of the 5 highest-churn pages (FM +1801 lines, dashboard +1495, torah +364, dictionary +131, flash-cards +122 since L's last boundary) read against the shipped UI. Hits: FM HowTo step 1 + its FAQ answer (Drawing mode absent from a closed enumeration, step named "Upload your letters"), dashboard Schedule Sync FAQ (describes the superseded single-day model), dictionary HowTo step 2 (Browse by Theme absent). **All 3 had a visible on-page FAQ twin carrying the same stale wording in EN and HE** — both halves fixed together (3237da8). - **theme-flipping-token-on-a-fixed-colour-plate (NEW, S191):** FIRST sweep 2026-08-10 (S191 Pass C). Surface: every text-bearing element of `torah_trainer.html` with the copy bar, bulk dock and settings drawer all open (all panels expanded), measured in **both** themes by alpha-compositing each node's colour over the nearest opaque ancestor background. **1 HIT — the bulk dock's `.btn-secondary` Clear at 1:1 in LIGHT only** (`var(--text)` = navy on the navy plate), fixed 6e3a57c; the page's other 21 `.btn-secondary` uses that were visible all passed. **Named unswept carriers** (files that contain both fixed navy plates and `.btn-secondary`, per a coarse count — the pairing still has to be measured, not grepped): `classroom_dashboard.html` (23 uses), `trope_tutor.html` (8), `hebrew_blend_generator.html` (5). The remaining pages carry navy plates but no `.btn-secondary`, so any hit there would come from a different token — `--muted` or `--text` on a gold button is the shape to look for. **2nd sweep 2026-08-11 (S193 Pass A).** Surface: the three carriers S191 named as unswept — dashboard (23 `.btn-secondary`), trope_tutor (8), generator (5) — same alpha-composite-over-nearest-opaque-ancestor method, both themes, all panels/drawers/`<details>` forced open; **653 elements measured in both themes, 18 pairs under 3:1**. **1 HIT: trope_tutor's 2 About-panel links at 1.99:1 in dark** (fixed cd52e15). **New sub-detector — run this FIRST, it is one page-evaluate and needs no compositing:** computed colour ∈ {`rgb(0, 0, 238)`, `rgb(85, 26, 139)`, `rgb(0, 0, 255)`} over every `<a>` in dark mode = an anchor no colour rule ever reached. Suite-wide: **7 anchors on 4 pages — 2 visible (fixed), 5 not rendered by default** (index `.ie-note`; the dictionary skip link; 3 FM `.hint` links, logged as a candidate since they render only once SVG-upload/English mode is on). **Two triage rules this sweep paid for:** (1) **filter `:disabled` before ranking** — the generator's 3 buttons at 1.84:1 dark are disabled, and `body.dark .btn:disabled` sets `#3d4f6a` at `opacity:1` on purpose (WCAG 1.4.3 exempts them); (2) **check whether the measured state is one the user ever sees** — the dictionary skip link is `clip`ped until `:focus`, and its `:focus` rule paints white-on-navy, so the reading was of a state with no viewer. **Deliberately not logged under this class:** 13 dashboard nodes under 3:1 with **flip ≈ 0** (brand gold on cream/white — bad in LIGHT, 8.7–11:1 dark). - **two-state-ready-flag-for-a-three-state-load**: FIRST sweep 2026-08-08 (S189, carried by Pass F). Detection caveats now in Pattern health: read the END state after ≥4 s, run BOTH languages, and check whether the first write precedes the fetch. **Re-swept 2026-08-11 (S193 Pass A): CLEAN.** All three S189 hits carry a failure flag now — `_emojiFailed` (flash_cards emoji tree), `_wordsFailed`/`_dictEmojiFailed` (the dictionary's two count readouts), `_realWordsPromise || _realWordsFailed` (the generator's Real Words caption). **Method note: the cheap re-sweep for an already-characterised carrier is static** — grep every single-truthiness gate on a lazily-fetched corpus and read whether a failure flag participates. - **invalid-SVG-geometry-from-an-unclamped-difference (NEW, S187):** swept 2026-08-08 over all 21 `<rect` emitters in `Hebrew_Font_Maker.html`, the file that had just absorbed ~1,083 lines of Draw-mode code. Method: an `innerHTML` setter wrapper regexing `<rect[^>]*(?:width|height)="-` on every assignment, with a JS stack per hit — which is how the frequency became visible (4 assignments across draw/trace/align/anchors vs the **1** line Chromium prints, because it collapses repeats). 1 hit: `marginGuidesSVG`'s left zone at width −115.2 (EN/1280) / −103.36 (HE/800), fixed 74e0530, with the byte-identical `wideFormGuidesSVG` shape guarded in the same commit. Suite-wide the emitters are Font Maker 23, dashboard 3, generator 1, dictionary 1 — and the 5 non-FM ones are static icons with literal geometry, i.e. no difference to go negative. **Registry lesson: name a carrier from an emitter count, never from what a file appears to do.**

- silent-external-media-failure: **registered + first swept 2026-08-02 (S183)**, `torah_trainer.html` only. - print-trailing-dead-space: **registered + first swept 2026-08-02 (S182)**, `torah_trainer.html` only. Found 44px (Page view, Letter), 28px (interlinear + side-by-side), 6px less on A4. Post-fix 0.00px on all six combinations; 4 of 70 PDFs dropped a sheet, 0 gained one. **Coverage gap: 11 root pages unswept.**
- lazily-loaded-dependency-renders-an-empty-shell: **registered + first swept 2026-08-02 (S182)**, `torah_trainer.html` only. Method: abort the module origin at the network layer (the loop's Playwright harness already aborts every external origin, so this is the default state) and render each layout; a surviving column header / row label with no content is the hit. One hit, fixed af90983 + an on-screen chip. **Coverage gap: every other lazy-CDN consumer in the suite.**
- print-media-leak / var-chain-overridden-by-a-literal: **2nd instance found + fixed 2026-08-02 (S182)** — `.tt-grid{align-items:start}`, correct on screen (the grid items are the 3 column wrappers) but wrong in print, where `display:contents` promotes every CELL to a grid item and the row border then draws at 2–3 different heights. Generalises the pattern: it is not only `@media (max-width:N)` blocks leaking to paper, but **any screen-correct declaration whose meaning changes because print restructures the box tree**. Still swept on `torah_trainer.html` only.
- print-media-leak / var-chain-overridden-by-a-literal: **registered + first swept 2026-08-02 (S181 Pass G)** over `torah_trainer.html`'s full stylesheet. 3 `@media (max-width:…)` blocks (600/900/**720**px); the 720px one was unqualified and reached paper. Method that found it: computing printable content width per paper×margin instead of assuming print is wide — A4@default = 718px < 720px while Letter@default = 740px, so **it is width-driven, not paper-driven** (`A4@none` 794px prints correctly; `Letter@0.5in` 720px prints wrong). **The other 11 root pages have NOT been swept for this** — do it in the next A.

- **H teacher walkthrough (S180, 2026-08-02):** `torah_trainer.html`, its **2nd-ever H and the first since S77 — 103 sessions, the stalest-H tool by a wide margin** (flash_cards S120, generator S130, trope_tutor S140, dashboard S107, Font Maker S163, dictionary S173). Run as **4 parallel lesson-prep scenarios** — bar-mitzvah setup, printed class handout, copying verses into a worksheet, cold first-week-of-school setup — plus per-finding adversarial verification. **3 of 4 scenarios reported** (the handout agent died mid-run; its print path was covered anyway by the top finding's own print-time receipts). **~28 findings and 12 seeds — the queue was empty for six sessions and is now full.** **Highest-value find came from following a walkthrough claim further than the walkthrough did:** the reported "maqaf-joined words weld together" turned out to share a root cause with a karaoke defect nobody had reported — `NIKKUD_RE` covers U+05B0–U+05C7, which includes maqaf/paseq/sof-pasuk/nun-hafukha, so hiding nikkud drops the sung-word count 27→24 and every word after the first maqaf highlights against the wrong audio timing. **Two scenarios INDEPENDENTLY found the fullscreen font-size override**, which is the strongest signal this pass produced. **Harness notes:** Sefaria/PocketTorah are unreachable from this box (`curl` exit 56 / CONNECT 403), so the loaded-reading paths need `page.route` **fulfil** with canned v3-shaped JSON — every agent that got real results did this; and the student-handout override enters and EXITS around `window.print()`, so a post-click DOM read shows the restored page — stub `window.print` and snapshot INSIDE it to see what actually prints.

- **A recurring-pattern sweep + A2 (S179, 2026-08-01):** the ~17 ACTIVE classes over `30d653f..HEAD` (74 commits, +822 net / 1,755 added lines, 16 files) — the first sweep ever to cover the four out-of-band **Learning Path** features (progress-report standing line, `?path=1` deep link, landscape certificate, certificate ceremony) that landed directly on `main`. **Run as a 5-way parallel workflow + a per-finding adversarial verify pass: 31 findings, 23 verified before the workflow died with the session pause, 0 REFUTED, 5 corrected to PARTIAL** (scope/consequence overstatements, never invented defects) — the results were recovered from the run journal, which is the ledgered S178 method note working as intended. **6 classes CLEAN with receipts** (unescaped-input/unsafe-parse, destructive-bulk, referenced-but-unauthored, double-localization, modal-focus-trap, dead-feature-guard); **11 classes hit.** **First-ever full A2 of all six retired patterns in one pass — all six stayed dead** (falsy-zero: 18 `||` in added lines hand-triaged, 0 in a numeric/boolean restore path; shadowed-global, listener/interval, workMode, undo-wiring, slider-commit: 0 matches each over the 611 added lines). **Two ledger 'not yet swept' backlogs were retired by doing them:** parse-per-call's 4 candidate stores (2 hit, 2 clean) and untrusted-shape-on-read's 4 stores (2 hit, 2 clean incl. the `ftRead` guard CONFIRMED rather than assumed). **Method worth reusing:** (a) the i18n miss-detector now serves `/js/i18n.js` through a `page.route` fulfill with a recorder in `t()` and counts a miss only when `dictLoaded=1`, which finally separates real gaps from the runtime's own pre-ready window; (b) the localStorage enumeration resolved 14 **constant-indirected** keys, and that is exactly where its one hit lived — a literal-only grep would have reported clean; (c) shape-seeding must use `page.addInitScript`, not post-load `setItem`, or init has already run.
