FULLY CLEAN: 24 cells (12 pages × EN/HE), on load and after one real interaction per tool — 0 pageerror, 0 console error, 0 console warning, 0 failed local request, 0 unhandled rejection, 0 missing key** (S171's double-localization detector folded in per that pointer). `serviceWorkers: 'block'` for determinism. **Round 1 was discarded: every interaction used a guessed selector, and receipts then showed 3 had proved nothing** (index opened no modal; FM matched 0 tiles — `.ltile`, not `.tile`; torah's English-only text lookup never clicked in Hebrew). **Known blind spot for the next B: it does not enable the FM trop mark panel, which is where 28 `referenced-but-unauthored` warnings live.**
- first-load & empty-state (Pass I): 2026-07-29 — **S171 — 14th run, first over S162–S170 and the first ever over the out-of-band Learner Ladder + printable progress report.** S161's gates re-run and **24/24 cells clean** (12 pages × light/1280 + dark/800: 0 pageerror, 0 raw i18n keys in text or in `title`/`aria-label`/`placeholder`, 0 failed local requests). **Added a new gate: 280 first-run button clicks across 8 tools → 0 pageerror, 0 throws** (print/navigation/native dialogs neutralised, `confirm` forced false so nothing destructive runs). Empty states verified, not assumed. **3 findings, all fixed** (ladder copy, geolocation alert, missing-key warnings). **2 harness artifacts killed before they could be reported** — see the Done entry; the load-bearing lesson is that a teardown which REMOVES nodes between clicks manufactures "Cannot set properties of null".
- performance (Pass D): 2026-07-29 — **S170 — flash_cards.html, 3rd dedicated D (S15→S75→S170) and the stalest D tool in loop history at 95 sessions; first D ever over the Learner Ladder, printable progress report and cut-out card sheet.** **1 hit (the profiles re-parse, see Done), everything else clean at 1×:** boot 67–71 ms max longtask (@4× 217–272 ms, throttled-only), `generateDeck(200)` 0.9–15.6 ms across all six modes, `renderCurrentCard` 0.7 ms avg / 2.7 ms max, `flipCard` ~0 ms, a real 12-card UI walk with 0 longtasks >200 ms, `printCardSheet` 46 pages / 401 cells in 4.7 ms, `_reportEnter` 3–5 ms. **Harness lessons: a synchronous loop cannot drive the 220 ms `markCorrect`→`autoAdvance` path (the first drill numbers were meaningless), and a suspicious 0 must be verified before it is reported (`#fcReport` vs `#fcReportSheet`).**
- **S165 (2026-07-27): `pre-ready-i18n / never-re-rendered` hit a 3rd time, and the test harness itself was found unsound for failure paths.** Pass F swept failure behaviour across all seven tools: the dictionary's parse-time fetch threw a ReferenceError in its own `.catch` (deferred `js/i18n.js`), and torah_trainer's empty state showed raw keys because `applyI18n` re-rendered the reading only when one was loaded. **Two sub-lessons worth keeping.** (1) Healing a stuck key from the *visible text* is a half-fix — the line then stops following language switches; record the key at write time and re-resolve from it. (2) **Service workers defeat `page.route`**: `sw.js` uses `skipWaiting()`+`clients.claim()`, so `/data/` comes from `DATA_CACHE` and never reaches the route handler. Any offline/failure test must open the context with `serviceWorkers: 'block'`; without it the test reports the happy path while appearing to test failure. This manufactured a "Flash Cards is silent" finding that the maintainer then chose at gate 1 — caught before any code was written, by instrumenting the writes instead of trusting the observation. **Every previous route-abort-based "offline" result in this ledger should be treated as unverified for the same reason.**

- **S164 (2026-07-27): the S157 `nameless-adjacent-text-labeled control` pattern was NOT clean — re-opened.** Pass E itself came back 6/6 with one licences finding, but chasing the S163 mark-editor candidate surfaced **20 nameless `input[type=range]` in the Font Maker**: the mark, precomposed-form and anchor panels build sliders by hand, so both S125's `vSlider()` pass and S157's sweep missed them. 13 fixed from their own on-line label (zero new copy); 7 logged for per-control decisions. **The detection lesson is the valuable part:** an initial headless audit of *rendered* sliders saw 12 and reported **1** nameless — the owning panels only render with specific state (selected mark / precomp target / anchor stage). Static-scan the source **and** drive each owning render function; a rendered-only sweep under-counted 20 as 1. Second method note, from the licences dimension: neither a loose substring test nor an exact-hostname test is correct — the loose one cleared the real gap (`files.readme.io`, on the word "files") and the strict one flagged five origins that are documented by service name. Confirm each by hand.

- **S163 (2026-07-26): two NEW patterns registered and swept to zero.** (1) `placeholder-as-only-accessible-name` — the S161 P4 recorded 2 instances; the real class was **17 across 6 tools**, found by computing the ACCNAME precedence chain in-page on all 11 pages rather than grepping. All fixed with **zero new CSV keys** (gate-2: "reuse existing strings everywhere"), names verified in Chromium's real AX tree **with a value typed in**, EN+HE. (2) `class-only-selected-state` — 20 selector buttons (19 font-maker, 1 flash_cards) whose selected state was a CSS class only; all fixed, all other tools swept clean. Pass L ran first and was structurally clean (see the Done entry). **Method notes from this session — the harness manufactured FOUR false findings before any were believed:** (a) an attribute regex must **backreference the opening quote**, or an apostrophe inside a `"…"` value truncates the match (this alone invented "trope_tutor description = 40 chars"); (b) `decode(null) → ''` conflated *absent* with *present-but-empty* and invented 4 phantom empty-tag findings; (c) reading Chromium's AX tree for a control inside a **collapsed panel** returns "not in AX tree" — reveal the ancestor chain first, and note a `type=number` input silently rejects a non-numeric test value; (d) stripping `.overlay.open` on load **destroys the Font Maker's restore prompt**, which read as "autosave is broken". Every one was caught by confirming against the real DOM/source before reporting — the third session running that this discipline paid off.

an existing-but-unwired key is invisible to both). **Detector for Pass K to adopt:** parse `ui-strings.csv`, then in a headless HE-locale run walk every text node and flag any whose text exactly equals a key's `en` where `he` differs **and no ancestor carries a `data-i18n*` attribute** (the ancestor check is essential — without it a translated parent block yields ~70% false positives). Cross-check with a literal `grep` of the key name across all html/js to separate never-wired keys from dynamically-built ones (`'prefix.'+name` construction is a known false positive of the grep half — the generator has literally zero `glyphname` references, which is what made its case conclusive). Only **5** files carry the class at all, 4 of which lacked the rule. The claim came from a grep for the string rather than a count of the CSS rule — the same adjacency-vs-count error the `invalid-SVG` entry above records, in a different guise, in the same session.
### Discovery-pass rotation (run one per session, stalest first)
- K i18n / localization audit: 2026-08-14 (**S207**: 13th run — **both gates clean for the 6th consecutive K** + the 85-commit diff probe clean, so the value was again past the gates: the **runtime reverse-lookup detector** (12 pages × HE, 0 pageerrors) returned **73 raw → 5 fixed in-pass (1684df2) + 2 logged**. Headline class, two carriers: **panels built before I18n.ready that applyI18n never rebuilds** — generator vowel-lock names (the 2026-07-29 audit's missed sibling) and the FM custom-glyph form behind `renderGrids`' once-guard ("wired" per the S177 census, yet never fired with live translations). Plus the kernclass Cancel (S192 fixed the same modal's TITLE — strict-visible detectors cannot see closed modals), the dictionary BETA badge, and the queued form-tab-intros family wired (+`<b>` CSV restore). **Method: the parent-only visibility filter is ancestor-blind** (computed display does not inherit none — every located hit was rect 0×0), and that accident is worth keeping: the hidden-DOM arm found what S192's strict-visible collector provably missed. Next K runs BOTH arms deliberately, then a reveal probe to split stale-forever from rebuilt-on-reveal.) Prior: 2026-08-11 (**S192**: 12th run — **both gates clean for the 5th consecutive K** (Check A clean + 14 baselined; Check B clean, no CSV debt), so the entire value was in the blind spot and the ACTIVE-pattern sweep. **Blind-spot probe over the never-K-swept delta `c88da45..HEAD` (126 commits, ~4,390 added HTML lines): 0 real finds**, reached only after two probe rewrites — v1's two-word prose filter rejected the exact shape S177 had found (single-word visible labels in template literals) — and four dismissals that were each GROUNDED rather than assumed: static attrs with their `data-i18n-*` siblings; `_pT` fallbacks (**all 4 keys verified present**); the FM changelog block (`HELP_CONTENT.about` is the by-design English fallback, and **`fontmaker.changelog.v5_8`/`v5_9` verified present + translated** — the out-of-band FM releases wired their i18n correctly); and the out-of-band dashboard `fmtClock()`'s hardcoded `'en-US'` (**all 6 locale calls on the page hardcode it**, including the pre-existing big clock — projected content follows `headerLang`, not `I18n.lang`). **The methodological result is the point of this run: the static orphaned-key census failed for the THIRD time and is now formally superseded.** S166 got 721/2/424; this session got **1082, then 677** after generalizing wildcard expansion to catch prefixes assigned to a variable before use (`const k = 'fontmaker.glyphname.' + slug`) — still swamped by dynamically-keyed families. **Replaced with a runtime reverse-lookup detector** (load each page in the Hebrew UI, collect the English still on screen, match it back against `en.json` keeping only keys whose `he.json` differs), which measures the user-visible symptom, is immune to dynamic keys, and hands you the candidate key with every hit. **35 hits / 12 pages / 0 pageerrors → 8 real after triage**; **Method notes new in S211:**
(1) **A consistency probe deserves the same scepticism as a bug report — in BOTH directions.** Pass F's three self-caught defects split as one false FAILURE (a laggard that was a fixture artifact) and one false CLEAN SWEEP (12/12 passes from a check that compared a value to itself). The clean sweep is the more dangerous of the two, because nothing about it invites a second look. Before believing any all-clean consistency result, ask what the probe would have to do to FAIL.
(2) **A toggle test must toggle an ODD number of times.** Exercising Enter and then Space to prove both work leaves the widget exactly where it started — so any downstream persistence/reload assertion silently compares a value to itself and passes everywhere. Separate the "both keys work" check from the "state survives" check.
(3) **A debounced save and a `page.reload()` are a race the reload wins.** torah's `saveSettings` is a 300 ms `setTimeout`, and navigating cancels the pending timer, so a persistence probe that reloads immediately reports "resets" for code that works perfectly. Wait past the debounce (grep the save function for its delay first) before any reload-based persistence check.
(4) **Assert the MECHANISM, not just the outcome.** The dashboard contrast fix's first cut used a 0.4 luminance cutoff and changed nothing at all — `#c8a000` sits at 0.373. It was caught in one run only because the probe printed the `ink-dark` CLASS alongside the ratio; a ratio-only probe would have shown "still 2.48" with no clue whether the rule was wrong, the class unapplied, or the selector mis-specified. When a fix has a trigger and an effect, measure both.
(5) **Prefer thresholds that are derived, not chosen.** Replacing that 0.4 with 0.30 was not tuning: white text scores (1.05)/(L+0.05), which crosses the 3:1 large-text bar at exactly L=0.30. A derived constant can be defended in a comment, survives someone adding an eighth day colour, and does not need re-tuning when the palette moves.
(6) **When a candidate says "re-derive before assuming", do exactly that — and be willing to close it.** The dashboard's six-taps-seven-cells receipt did not reproduce, and grounding showed all three mechanisms that could have produced it (`paintCell` idempotence, `addWeekPeriod`'s null push, `sortWeekPeriods` permuting cells with periods) are individually clean. Closing a stale finding with a receipt is a real iteration outcome, not a wasted one.
**Method notes new in S210:**
(1) **An EN-vs-HE rendered-body-length ratio is a cheap whole-page localization detector.** It needs no key census, catches an entire page in one measurement, and distinguishes untranslated-vs-content-only by counting Hebrew-bearing blocks. terms.html's 6199→6198 (a ONE-character delta, against privacy.html's 7199→6112) is what surfaced the finding.
(2) **`data-i18n` coverage is not translation coverage.** terms.html carries 35 `data-i18n` attributes and passes every structural check; the defect lived entirely in the CSV's empty `he` cells, which Check B reports **warn-only**. A green i18n gate is not evidence a page speaks Hebrew.
(3) **A selector that names the thing can still match the wrong thing.** `.legend` matched the *grid* legend ("image/traced/anchored") rather than `#stageLegend`, manufacturing four confident false passes — the S209 distinctive-substring lesson recurring. Prefer the id the render function itself writes to (locate it by reading the function, not by guessing the class).
(4) **Stub the timing, not the outcome, when proving a render-count fix.** The translit differential only held because the stub ESM carried a *controllable import delay*: with an instant stub, both baseline and fixed render once and the bug is invisible. Fulfil the CDN with a delay, and run the slow case too — that is what proves the fallback path survived.
(5) **When a candidate's every slice needs new copy, an unattended gate makes it unpickable.** The torah failed-reading dead end (P3, reproduced live by this pass) had to be passed over for its non-copy sibling; prefer logic-shaped candidates in unattended sessions and save copy-shaped ones for the gate batch.
**Queued and needing no gate:** (1) the **FM backlog's last 3 keys** — letter_drop/markctl_drop need the 2 kind keys ("an SVG"/"an image"), side_positions_kept needs the .one/.other transcription of the live possessive ternary (no new wording; HE drafts REVIEW-marked); (2) the **NEW S209 P4** — the anchors-preview banner literal (needs 1 authored key); (3) the dictionary font-license nested-interactive span (the haul's one deliberately-parked item).
**Gate queue for the next attended batch:** the two S180 splits — the parsha **deferred-fetch flow** (P3, gate-2: changes the pick-a-parsha default) and the **font-panel/size-slider cross-reference** (P4, gate-2: wording/IA). **Gate-1 lead unchanged: the dashboard weekly-grid print seed** (offered twice, skipped twice, never struck). **Maintainer review queue: 3 REVIEW-marked HE drafts** (flashcards.sheet.practiced/_tip S207, fontmaker.status.template_saved S208).
**Method notes new in S209:**
(1) **A regex census cannot see helper-CONSTRUCTED keys.** The fresh RFC-4180 census flagged the 5 anchor_* keys as unwired because anchorLabelT builds `'fontmaker.panels.anchor_' + slug` at runtime — a literal-argument regex sees nothing. Cross-check every "unwired" hit against the file's known key-building helpers (the ledger's dynamic-reachable list was already right); the 2 kern_export_summary plurals are the same lesson dot-side.
(2) **The FM wizard fixture defaults trop OFF, and gotoAnchors('trop') falls back to nikkud silently** — call setMarkEnabled('trop', true) first, and remember the anchor-panel hints render only with an ACTIVE mark (click one in the grid). Also the workspace left panel is `#ctlLeft` (.ws-left) — a `.ws-side` selector silently falls through to body-text matching, where substring collisions (vowel vs trop hint texts) manufacture false passes; match on DISTINCTIVE substrings.
(3) **Verify BOTH state signals on rebuilt DOM.** The chant button's template carried state-true TEXT but dropped the .active CLASS on every fetchAndRender rebuild — a class-only probe read "not engaged" while the user-visible label was correct. When a button's state renders in two channels (label + class), assert both; fixing the template to render both is the durable shape.
(4) **A "N small ones" polish-set candidate can hide gated members** — ground each item before treating the set as one mechanical iteration: of the S180 four, one was already resolved (stale), one was mechanical, and two were gate-2 (default-flow and wording changes). Splitting them into their own gated candidate lines keeps the set honest.
**Method notes new in S208:**
(1) **PDF geometry receipts need a CTM walker + /Pages /Kids order.** Raw `Tm`/`re` operands sit under per-block `cm` transforms (Chromium prints at a 3.125× internal scale with per-run translations), file-scan `/Type /Page` order is NOT reading order, and content streams carry **clip-culled neighbour-page text** that a naive above-content-top filter counts as band content (the walker proved it: page 1 = exactly the band's 12 runs; pages 2+ = those 12 + 8 culled body runs). This **retroactively qualifies the S207 band-rect receipt** — right conclusion, but its y-filter happened to match device-space rects; future print receipts walk the CTM, order pages by /Kids, and window to the band's own geometric strip.
(2) **Choose fixture parshiot so the aliyah starts fall inside the fixture's refs.** parshaSelect index 1 is Noach (starts 6:9+ — zero matches against a chapters-1/2 fixture, so 0 dividers render and a divider-dependent receipt silently degrades); index 0 Bereshit puts 2:4/2:20 inside a two-chapter [120,80] fixture. S207's verify_band used Noach harmlessly (no divider dependency) — divider staging is what exposed it.
(3) **One remapped token can converge a whole dark-accent leak class.** Before adding per-selector print re-inks for `body.dark` rules, check whether every PRINTABLE reader of the token wants the same paper value — `--gold-light`'s only background uses were :hover states, so print-remapping the token itself (→ text-gold) fixed nn-val, the holiday line, omer, weather-temp, and text links in one line.
(4) **The dense-sidebar visibility-wait class recurred** (S202/S207's note holds): `<option>`s and collapsed-panel content need `state:'attached'`, and a dynamically-rendered error affordance can hang off a DIFFERENT ancestor than its happy-path sibling (the weather ↺ Refresh lives under `#weatherBody`, not `#weatherBlock`) — hide-list selectors must target the common ancestor.
**Method notes new in S207:**
(1) **The parent-only computed-style visibility filter is ancestor-blind** — `getComputedStyle(child).display` does NOT inherit an ancestor's `display:none`, so every "visible" hit must be re-proven by rect area / `checkVisibility()`. But the blind arm is worth running deliberately: hidden-DOM English finds stale boot renders and closed-modal unwired strings that a strict-visible collector (S192) provably missed. Run BOTH arms, then a reveal probe to split stale-forever (real) from rebuilt-on-reveal (benign).
(2) **A render-once guard defeats applyI18n's re-render coverage** (`if (grid) renderGrid(); else renderPanel()`): the panel half keeps its build-time language forever while the key census counts it "wired". Sweep i18n-bearing builders for once-guards; the fix is an explicit full rebuild in applyI18n with the form-reset trade documented in place.
(3) **position:fixed + an enlarged @page top margin = a per-sheet running header that cannot overlap content** — verifiable without pdftotext by decompressing PDF content streams (object-boundary splitting, then slice by the declared `/Length` — `rstrip` can eat a trailing 0x0A that is REAL deflate data) and counting the band's ops above the content-top y on every page.
(4) **`waitForSelector` needs `state:'attached'` for `<option>`s and collapsed-panel content** (hit twice this session — the S202 visibility-wait class keeps recurring).
**Method notes new in S206:**
(1) **fixed-position overlays have offsetParent === null.** An offsetParent-based visibility filter silently drops every open modal — three of this pass's four false alarms trace to that one probe shape. Filter on getComputedStyle position + display instead.
(2) **Ground a dead-end candidate against the button's render path before converting its alert.** The queued weakness alert() was unreachable (the render hard-disables the button); the real fix was disabled→aria-disabled + a stated inline reason — the Start-button shape the file itself documents.
(3) **The ?s= emit side can reuse the paste-code encoder byte-for-byte** — emit the URL-safe alphabet the decoder already tolerates and the link path adds zero new parsing surface (and the words-inline embed travels for free).
